==Description==
The '''Ultra Autocannon/10''' (also '''Ultra AC/10''' or '''UAC/10''') was the second largest of the [[Ultra autocannon]]s. First introduced into the [[Inner Sphere]] by the [[Clans]], [[Free Worlds League]] scientists were the first to reverse engineer the weapon and make it viable for mass-production, though the knowledge quickly spread to the other [[Successor States]]. Ultra autocannons were capable of firing at twice the rate of their [[autocannon∕10|standard counterpart, doubling heat and—potentially—damage.

==Variants==
In the years between the [[Second Exodus]] and the beginning of [[Operation KLONDIKE]], the nascent [[Clan]]s began development of what would become the Ultra AC/10. Experimental prototype versions of this new weapon were used during Operation KLONDIKE in small quantities. The Clan prototype Ultra AC/10 available during this period had identical performance to the later Inner Sphere Ultra AC/10, though the latter required additional mounting space and jammed more frequently.<ref name="H:OKp156">''Historical: Operation KLONDIKE'', pp. 156–157: "Prototype Clan Systems"</ref>

==Models==
The Ultra autocannon/10 was manufactured on the following planets:

===Imperator Automatic===
After producing an [[Ultra autocannon∕5|Ultra version]] of the [[autocannon∕5]] for many years, developing an Ultra AC/10 model was a natural project for Atreus-based [[Imperator Automatic Weaponry]] during the initial [[Clan Invasion era]]. With the company testing the new system in mid-[[3056]], the [[Free Worlds League Military]] field-tested several prototypes during [[Operation GUERRERO]] with the FWLM approving the weapon for general use and IAW able to bring the first Inner Sphere-technology Ultra AC/10 to market in [[3057]].<ref name=FMFWLp138>''Field Manual: Free Worlds League'', pp. 138–139: "Ultra AC/10"</ref><ref name=ER3062p104>''Era Report: 3062'', p. 104: "Ultra Autocannons"</ref>

''[[Mandrissa]]'' [[Elaine Jackson]] of [[Capellan Confederation]]-based [[Bithinian Ballistics]] would secure a deal with IAW to obtain plan for their weapon to manufacture it for the [[Capellan Confederation Armed Forces]], though rumor holds her own staff brokered the deal only for her to fire them and steal the credit.<ref name=HBHLp133>''Handbook: House Liao'', p. 133: "Bithinian Ballistics"</ref>

==Description==
The '''Ultra Autocannon/2''' (also '''Ultra AC/2''' or '''UAC/2''') was the smallest bore autocannon of the [[Ultra autocannon|Ultra series]]. Ultra autocannons were capable of firing at twice the rate of their [[autocannon∕2|standard counterpart, doubling heat and—potentially—damage. The Ultra autocannon/2 also had the longest range and most ammo per ton of any Ultra autocannon; however, it also dealt the least amount of damage.

==Variants==
In the years between the [[Second Exodus]] and the beginning of [[Operation KLONDIKE]], the nascent [[Clan]]s began development of what would become the Ultra AC/2. Experimental prototype versions of this new weapon were used during Operation KLONDIKE in small quantities. The Clan prototype Ultra AC/2 available during this period had identical performance to the later Inner Sphere Ultra AC/2, though the latter took up additional mounting space and was more prone to jam.<ref name="H:OKp156">''Historical: Operation Klondike'', pp. 156–157: "Prototype Clan Systems"</ref>

==Models==
The Ultra autocannon/2 was manufactured on the following planets in the [[Inner Sphere]]:

==Description==
The '''Ultra autocannon/20''' (also '''Ultra AC/20''' or '''UAC/20''') was the largest bore [[Ultra autocannon]]. Like all Ultra autocannons, it was capable of twice the rate of fire of a standard autocannon, doubling its heat output and—potentially—its damage. Only weaponry like the [[heavy Gauss rifle]] or [[HAG 40]] could match the sheer damage output of the Ultra AC/20.

==Variants==
In the years between the [[Second Exodus]] and the beginning of [[Operation KLONDIKE]], the nascent [[Clan]]s began development of what would become the Ultra autocannon/20. Experimental prototype versions of this new weapon were used during Operation KLONDIKE in small quantities. The Clan prototype Ultra autocannon/20 available during this period had identical performance to the later Inner Sphere Ultra AC/20, though the latter required additional mounting space and was more prone to jamming.<ref name="H:OKp156">''Historical: Operation Klondike'', pp. 156–157: "Prototype Clan Systems"</ref>

==Description==
Introduced in [[2640]] by the [[Terran Hegemony]],<ref name=TMp290/> the '''Ultra Autocannon/5''' (also '''Ultra AC/5''' or '''UAC/5''') was an [[Ultra Autocannon|upgraded version]] of the standard [[autocannon∕5]]. Utilizing a different loading mechanism, the Ultra autocannons were capable of maintaining a substantially increased rate of fire over traditional or [[LB-X autocannon]]s, at the cost of higher heat and the risk of jamming. The rapid-fire autocannons required a much larger supply of ammunition to maintain their rate of fire, limiting their effectiveness. The Ultra autocannon/5 had an increased range, at the cost of reduced damage over higher-class versions.

==Variants==
*; UAC/P: During the [[War of 3039]], the [[Federated Commonwealth]] employed a prototype version of the Ultra AC/5 among some of its elite units.  These systems were similar to standard Ultra AC/5s, except they required an additional critical slot, jammed more often when operating at its maximum rate of fire, and could jam even when fired at the standard rate.

==Notes==
* This article refers to a common object and addresses the object as a common noun. The preferred term is "Ultra autocannon/5" for the first usage as, per the [[BattleTechWiki:Manual_of_Style#Acronyms_and_abbreviations|manual of style]] abbreviations should only be used following the full spelling of the word. "Autocannon" should only be capitalised in section titles & table headings. Otherwise, as a common noun, it remains lower case.

==Description==
The '''Vehicular Grenade Launcher''' is a one-shot area-effect weapon commonly attached to [[combat vehicles]], though they may also be carried by a wide variety of machines, from [[BattleMechs]] and [[conventional fighter]]s to [[small craft]] and [[DropShips]].  Their popularity dropped off with the advent of 'Mechs and their limited one-shot nature, however their utility as an anti-infantry or defensive armament has ensured their continued use on modern units.

The most common type of [[grenade]] launched is the fragmentation grenade, showering the target area in deadly shrapnel.  Other types include smoke grenades for creating concealment and incendiary grenades for setting fires.  The [[Draconis Combine]] introduced chaff grenades in [[3052]], which release a cloud of electromagnetic dust and high-intensity flares to confuse sensors, though their short duration means traditional [[ECM|electronic jamming]] remains the more popular choice.

## Weapon: Category:Rifles (Cannon)
### Data Extract Profile
Not to be confused with describing handheld long arm sized weaponry, these vehicle sized "rifles" are the precursors to modern [[autocannon]] and were based on the main guns used by tanks on pre-spaceflight [[Terra]]. They are generally described as being "light", "medium" or "heavy", rather than the usual convention of describing autocannon by the amount of damage they do. 




---

## Weapon: Category:Special Munitions
### Data Extract Profile
'''Special Munitions''' is the generic name given to specialty [[Autocannon]] ammunition. Typically they offer increased damage against one or more types of battlefield unit at the expense of cost and weight. Special munitions can only be used by standard or [[Light Autocannon|light autocannon]].

For alternate missile launcher ammunition, see the [[:Category:Alternate Ammunition|Alternate Ammunition]] category.
